      description: 'Collect passport with sticker visa and register address with OGIM or online within 10 business days of arrival in Belarus.'
    }
  ],
  specialRequirements: {
    entry_rules: 'Mandatory registration with the Department of Citizenship and Migration (OGIM) or online via portal.gov.by within 10 business days for stays exceeding 10 days in private accommodation. Valid travel insurance policy covering at least €10,000 must be presented at border control upon entry.'
  }
};

    3. Gather Documentation: Collect signed VOW application form, photos, financial statements, travel insurance (€30k coverage), flight reservations, and proof of stay.
    4. Submit Biometrics & Pay Fees: Attend appointment at VFS Global to submit biometrics (fingerprints & photo) and pay consular fee (€90) and service fee.
    5. Track & Receive Passport: Track status online via VFS and collect passport with Schengen visa sticker upon processing completion.
- `specialRequirements`:
    - `entry_rules`: 'Adheres strictly to the Schengen 90/180-day rule. Travel insurance must cover minimum €30,000 for emergency medical costs and emergency repatriation. Proof of sufficient funds is mandatory (€95/day for hotel stay or €45/day if staying with host).'

    },

      mandatory: true
    }
  ],

    {
      step: 2,

      step: 3,

      description: 'Attend the VFS centre to submit your physical application bundle, valid original passport, and enrol biometric identifiers.'
    },
    {
      step: 4,
      title: 'Track Application & Receive Passport',
      description: 'Track processing progress online. Once adjudicated by the Embassy of Ireland, collect your passport containing the visa sticker from VFS or via courier.'
    }
  ],
  specialRequirements: {
    entry_rules: 'Ireland is NOT part of the Schengen Area. Schengen visas are not valid for entry to Ireland. Indian nationals traveling on a standard tourist visa must obtain a dedicated Short Stay C Irish Visa. Under the British-Irish Visa Scheme (BIVS), travelers with an eligible UK visitor visa endorsed with "BIVS" may visit Ireland after first clearing UK border control, but direct travel from India to Ireland requires an Irish visa.'
  }
};

  }

    }

    },
    {
      step: 2,
      title: 'Prepare Documentation Dossier',

      title: 'Schedule VFS Appointment & Submit Application',

Travel health insurance must provide minimum €30,000 emergency coverage without deductibles.'
  }

Retain all post office deposit receipts as proof of legal residence until the physical residence card is issued.'
  }
};

export default {
  country: 'Russia',
  fromCountry: 'India',
  visaCategory: 'Tourist Visa (Unified eVisa / Sticker Visa)',
  authority: 'Ministry of Foreign Affairs of the Russian Federation (MFA)',
  channels: [
    'Unified eVisa (electronic-visa.kdmid.ru)',
    'Russian Visa Application Centre (VFS Global)',
    'Consular Section of Russian Embassy'
  ],
  processingTime: {
    eVisa: '4 calendar days (Statutory maximum)', // Confirmed by multiple sources
    standardSticker: '7-20 working days', // Updated based on search results
    expressSticker: '3-5 working days' // Confirmed by search results
  },
  fees: {
    eVisaTotal: 'Approx. 52 USD (~₹4,300 - ₹5,054 INR)', // Confirmed by multiple sources
    stickerConsularStandard: 'Single Entry: ₹6,720; Double Entry: ₹10,752; Multiple Entry: ₹20,160 (for 4-20 working days processing)', // Updated with specific consular fees
    vfsServiceFee: 'Applicable for in-person sticker submission (Approx. ₹1,200 - ₹2,500)' // Added approximate VFS fee
  },
